- **ants**: Structure implementation as small, atomic, traceable changes with one owner, one observable signal and an updated task record. Use when planning, implementing, reviewing or refactoring code, especially to avoid big patches, unrelated refactors, duplicated state or unclear ownership. ([PierreDillard/agent-workflow-kit](https://github.com/PierreDillard/agent-workflow-kit/tree/HEAD/modules/tasks/skills/ants) · ★1)
- **ants**: Send messages to ants (other coding agents on the NATS bus) and read replies with the ants CLI. Use when asked to ask, message, delegate to or check on an ant or the messenger, post to or read an ants channel, react to a message, answer an ant's question, inspect the ants inbox, colony, escalations, tool approvals or thread notes, start, follow, stop or resume a trail run, report what ants spent, or read what an ant remembers. ([quanghle/ants](https://github.com/quanghle/ants/tree/HEAD/contrib/skills/ants))
- **ants-map-check-and-fix**: How to read the checker's findings for an Ants map (check_map) and fix them - whole hills, a hill and starting ants for every color, food piles inside the map, description length, the 90-row limit - and how to check by hand what the checker does not, that ants can walk from every hill to food. Use it when you are about to say a map is finished, and whenever check_map reports a finding. ([dchadd427/ants-cross-platform](https://github.com/dchadd427/ants-cross-platform/tree/HEAD/ai_skills/ants-map-check-and-fix) · ★2)
- **ants-map-guide**: Start here when a person asks for an Ants map (the 1998 real-time strategy game of four colored ant colonies) to be made or improved in the Map Maker. It gives the order of work from an idea to a checked map, what to ask first, what you may and may not do, and what to tell the person at the end. Use it together with the other ants-map skills and the Map Maker tools. ([dchadd427/ants-cross-platform](https://github.com/dchadd427/ants-cross-platform/tree/HEAD/ai_skills/ants-map-guide) · ★2)
- **ants-map-layout**: How to lay out a fair and fun Ants map for two to four colors - where to put hills and starting ants, how far apart, how to share food, where chokepoints and power-ups go, how many ants and eggs, how long a match should be - with numbers measured from the six original maps. Use it when you decide where things go, before you call put_hill, put_ants, put_food or place power-ups. ([dchadd427/ants-cross-platform](https://github.com/dchadd427/ants-cross-platform/tree/HEAD/ai_skills/ants-map-layout) · ★2)
- **ants-map-terrain**: How to shape the ground of an Ants map so that it looks right and plays right - the five kinds of ground, hard edges and edge pieces (fix_edges, auto_edges), the ring of mud beside water, strips that must be two cells wide, bridges, and natural shorelines. Use it before you paint lakes, mud, paths or any change of ground, and when the edges look wrong. ([dchadd427/ants-cross-platform](https://github.com/dchadd427/ants-cross-platform/tree/HEAD/ai_skills/ants-map-terrain) · ★2)
- **ants-map-vocabulary**: The words and numbers of Ants maps: the four colors, the five kinds of ground and their tile ids, objects, bigger objects, plants, power-ups, food items, ant types, the rules word, and the limits of Classic and Extended maps. Use it when you need a tile id, a name, or a size limit, or to understand what a tile in a map is. ([dchadd427/ants-cross-platform](https://github.com/dchadd427/ants-cross-platform/tree/HEAD/ai_skills/ants-map-vocabulary) · ★2)

Erlass aus persoenlichen oder sachlichen Billigkeitsgründen § 227 AO. Vollstreckungsaufschub bei unbilligem Vollstreckungserfolg § 258 AO Verzinsung bei Stundung § 234 AO. - **anyscale-on-aks-do-not-hardcode-a-gpu-e2627132c7a9a479**: Anyscale on AKS: do not hardcode a GPU SKU in Terraform. GPU pools are not created by default on purpose. Use when hitting this exact issue with Anyscale on AKS. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/anyscale-on-aks-do-not-hardcode-a-gpu-e2627132c7a9a479))
- **anyscale-on-eks-insufficientinstanceca-a5e6890f9dd17ab9**: Anyscale on EKS: InsufficientInstanceCapacity or vCPU limit errors on GPU node groups. Also check disk: on Bottlerocket node groups, container images or Ray object spill can fill the ~20 GiB AMI-default data volume silently, so size the data volume explicitly for training workloads. Use when hitting this exact issue with Anyscale on EKS. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/anyscale-on-eks-insufficientinstanceca-a5e6890f9dd17ab9))
- **anyscale-on-eks-the-operator-pod-needs-df30702ecba336ef**: Anyscale on EKS: the Operator pod needs its own IAM identity, not the node role. The fix is an EKS Pod Identity association with a dedicated role trusted by pods.eks.amazonaws.com, bound to the Operator's namespace and service account. Use when hitting this exact issue with Anyscale on EKS. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/anyscale-on-eks-the-operator-pod-needs-df30702ecba336ef))
- **anyscale-private-cluster-operator-cann-7f5815f77a9018dc**: Anyscale private cluster: Operator cannot reach the control plane over Private Link. Private Blob and DFS endpoints resolve and route inside the VNet, so run proof uploads from the Linux jump host rather than the workstation. Use when hitting this exact issue with Anyscale private cluster. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/anyscale-private-cluster-operator-cann-7f5815f77a9018dc))

([anysiteio/agent-skills](https://github.com/anysiteio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/anysite-gtm/skills/anysite-crm-prospect) · ★20)
